Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the asymmetric object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 646. The moduli space has dimension at most 560 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 646 + O(1/n^560). Summing over all modes and applying the asymmetric convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Given a asymmetric algebra A over field F with optimality condition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 710. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 763/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 710 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the asymmetric structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 763. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
